/home/idor/oe-local/oe-train/lib/python3.12/site-packages/numpy/f2py/tests/src/regression/AB.inc
# -*- mode: python ; coding: utf-8 -*-
"""PyInstaller spec for Kingdom OS binary.

Bundles: kingdom_os_entry.py + oe_engine/ + kernel/ + axioms/ + src/ + ontology/
Output: single-file executable 'kingdom-os' (Linux) or 'kingdom-os.exe' (Windows)
"""

import os
import glob

# Collect all domain invariants, ontology JSON, and SAL modules
datas = [
    ('ontology/ontology.json', 'ontology'),
    ('src/domains', 'src/domains'),
    ('axioms', 'axioms'),
    ('src/sal', 'src/sal'),
    ('kernel', 'kernel'),
    ('oe_engine', 'oe_engine'),
]

# Auto-generate hiddenimports for all domain modules.
# thinker.py uses importlib.import_module() dynamically, so PyInstaller
# cannot trace these imports automatically — they must be listed explicitly.
_domain_hidden_imports = []
for _inv in sorted(glob.glob("src/domains/*/invariants.py")):
    # e.g. "src/domains/d_aerospace/invariants.py" -> "src.domains.d_aerospace.invariants"
    _mod = _inv.replace(os.sep, ".").replace("/", ".").removesuffix(".py")
    _domain_hidden_imports.append(_mod)
    # Also include the domain package itself and implementation module
    _pkg = _mod.rsplit(".", 1)[0]  # e.g. "src.domains.d_aerospace"
    _domain_hidden_imports.append(_pkg)
    _impl_path = _inv.replace("invariants.py", "implementation.py")
    if os.path.exists(_impl_path):
        _domain_hidden_imports.append(_pkg + ".implementation")
    _domain_path = _inv.replace("invariants.py", "domain.py")
    if os.path.exists(_domain_path):
        _domain_hidden_imports.append(_pkg + ".domain")

a = Analysis(
    ['kingdom_os_entry.py'],
    pathex=['.'],
    binaries=[],
    datas=datas,
    hiddenimports=[
        'axioms.logic',
        'axioms.capability_security',
        'axioms.process_algebra',
        'kernel.boot',
        'kernel.scheduler',
        'kernel.memory_manager',
        'kernel.ipc',
        'kernel.anti_mimicry',
        'kernel.hal',
        'oe_engine.engine',
        'oe_engine.manifest',
        'oe_engine.router',
        'oe_engine.thinker',
        'oe_engine.speaker',
        'oe_engine.synthesizer',
        'oe_engine.generator',
        'oe_engine.conversation',
        'oe_engine.cli',
        'src.sal.cross_domain_adjunction',
        *_domain_hidden_imports,
    ],
    hookspath=[],
    hooksconfig={},
    runtime_hooks=[],
    excludes=['torch', 'numpy', 'scipy', 'pandas', 'matplotlib', 'sklearn',
              'seaborn', 'tqdm', 'requests', 'lxml', 'ijson', 'cryptography'],
    noarchive=False,
)

pyz = PYZ(a.pure, a.zipped_data)

exe = EXE(
    pyz,
    a.scripts,
    a.binaries,
    a.datas,
    [],
    name='kingdom-os',
    debug=False,
    bootloader_ignore_signals=False,
    strip=False,
    upx=False,
    console=True,
)

The 1B Llama model was **never trained**. CUDA was **never configured**. This has been true since January 30, 2026 and remains true today. [42-cite-0](#42-cite-0)

- Stage 3 (evaluation): **PENDING** [42-cite-1](#42-cite-1) [42-cite-2](#42-cite-2)

## Layer 3: Kimi CLI Stub Delivery (April 24, 2026)

The Forensic Offensive Campaign Part 5 created three "LoRA pipeline" tools.All three are **stubs**:

"""D_GAME_ENGINE_DEVELOPMENT invariants — Yeshua Standard. 0 floats.

Standards:
- IEEE 730 — Software Quality Assurance (game engine reliability)
- NIST SP 800-218 — Secure Software Development Framework
- ECS/DOTS architecture requirements (Unity, Bevy)
- IEEE 1074 — Software Development Life Cycle
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from fractions import Fraction
from typing import Dict, Tuple
from axioms.logic import ProofObject
from .implementation import PhysicsConfig, GameState, SaveFile


def check_physics_time_step_valid(config: PhysicsConfig) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:
    """Physics time step must be within valid simulation range.

    Standard: IEEE 730 — simulation stability requirements
    Falsifies if: time_step <= 0 or time_step > Fraction(1, 20).
    falsifies_if: time_step <= 0 or time_step > Fraction(1, 20).
    """
    max_step = Fraction(1, 20)
    ok = Fraction(0) < config.time_step <= max_step
    premises = [
        f"time_step={config.time_step}",
        f"max_step={max_step}",
    ]
    return ok, ProofObject(
        rule="PhysicsTimeStepValid",
        premises=premises,
        conclusion=f"PASS: time_step {config.time_step} in range" if ok else f"VIOLATION: time_step {config.time_step} not in (0, {max_step}]",
    )


def check_simulation_accuracy(config: PhysicsConfig) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:
    """Physics simulation accuracy must meet quality threshold.

    Standard: Game engine physics simulation — accuracy requirements for deterministic replay
    Falsifies if: simulation_accuracy < Fraction(9, 10).
    falsifies_if: simulation_accuracy < Fraction(9, 10).
    """
    threshold = Fraction(9, 10)
    ok = config.simulation_accuracy >= threshold
    premises = [
        f"gravity={config.gravity}",
        f"simulation_accuracy={config.simulation_accuracy}",
        f"threshold={threshold}",
    ]
    return ok, ProofObject(
        rule="SimulationAccuracy",
        premises=premises,
        conclusion=f"PASS: accuracy {config.simulation_accuracy} >= {threshold}" if ok else f"VIOLATION: accuracy {config.simulation_accuracy} < {threshold}",
    )


def check_physics_substep_efficiency(config: PhysicsConfig) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:
    """Physics substep count as fraction of hardware limit must not exceed 1.

    Standard: Bullet Physics / PhysX — substep efficiency requirements
    Falsifies if: max_substeps / 16 > Fraction(1).
    falsifies_if: max_substeps / 16 > Fraction(1).
    """
    hardware_limit = 16
    ok = config.max_substeps <= hardware_limit
    efficiency = Fraction(config.max_substeps, hardware_limit) if hardware_limit > 0 else Fraction(0)
    premises = [
        f"max_substeps={config.max_substeps}",
        f"hardware_limit={hardware_limit}",
        f"efficiency={efficiency}",
    ]
    return ok, ProofObject(
        rule="PhysicsSubstepEfficiency",
        premises=premises,
        conclusion=f"PASS: efficiency {efficiency} within limit" if ok elsef"VIOLATION: efficiency {efficiency} exceeds limit",
    )


def check_save_integrity_score(save: SaveFile) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:
    """Save file integrity score must meet data preservation threshold.

    Standard: NIST SP 800-218 — data integrity verification
    Falsifies if: checksum empty OR progression_fraction < Fraction(0).
    falsifies_if: checksum empty OR progression_fraction < Fraction(0).
    """
    has_checksum = bool(save.checksum.strip())
    progression_ok = save.progression_fraction >= Fraction(0)
    ok = has_checksum and progression_ok
    premises = [
        f"version={save.version}",
        f"checksum_present={has_checksum}",
        f"progression_fraction={save.progression_fraction}",
    ]
    return ok, ProofObject(
        rule="SaveIntegrityScore",
        premises=premises,
        conclusion="PASS: save integrity verified" if ok else "VIOLATION: save integrity failed",
    )


def check_game_state_consistency(state: GameState) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:
    """Game state consistency score must meet synchronization threshold.

    Standard: IEEE 730 — simulation state validity for multiplayer
    Falsifies if: state_consistency_score < Fraction(1, 2).
    falsifies_if: state_consistency_score < Fraction(1, 2).
    """
    threshold = Fraction(1, 2)
    ok = state.state_consistency_score >= threshold
    premises = [
        f"frame_number={state.frame_number}",
        f"object_count={len(state.objects)}",
        f"state_consistency_score={state.state_consistency_score}",
        f"threshold={threshold}",
    ]
    return ok, ProofObject(
        rule="GameStateConsistency",
        premises=premises,
        conclusion=f"PASS: consistency {state.state_consistency_score} >= {threshold}" if ok else f"VIOLATION: consistency {state.state_consistency_score} < {threshold}",
    )


def check_save_progression_valid(save: SaveFile) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:
    """Save file progression fraction must be within valid range [0, 1].

    Standard: Game progression invariants — bounded progression metric
    Falsifies if: progression_fraction < Fraction(0) OR progression_fraction > Fraction(1).
    falsifies_if: progression_fraction < Fraction(0) OR progression_fraction > Fraction(1).
    """
    ok = Fraction(0) <= save.progression_fraction <= Fraction(1)
    premises = [
        f"player_name={save.player_name}",
        f"level={save.level}",
        f"progression_fraction={save.progression_fraction}",
    ]
    return ok, ProofObject(
        rule="SaveProgressionValid",
        premises=premises,
        conclusion=f"PASS: progression {save.progression_fraction} in [0, 1]" if ok else f"VIOLATION: progression {save.progression_fraction} out of bounds",
    )


def run_all_invariants() -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Run all checks with nominal inputs. All must PASS.

    Standard: Game-engine-development nominal executable check set.
    Falsifies if: any check returns FAIL (nominal inputs should always pass).
    falsifies_if: any check returns FAIL (nominal inputs should always pass).
    """
    config = PhysicsConfig(
        gravity=Fraction(-98, 10),
        time_step=Fraction(1, 60),
        max_substeps=8,
        simulation_accuracy=Fraction(99, 100),
    )
    state = GameState(frame_number=1000, state_consistency_score=Fraction(1, 1))
    save = SaveFile(
        version="1.0",
        player_name="Alice",
        level=5,
        inventory=[],
        checksum="sha256:abc123",
        progression_fraction=Fraction(1, 2),
        inventory_value=Fraction(1, 1),
    )
    results = {}
    for fn, args in [
        (check_physics_time_step_valid, (config,)),
        (check_simulation_accuracy, (config,)),
        (check_physics_substep_efficiency, (config,)),
        (check_save_integrity_score, (save,)),
        (check_game_state_consistency, (state,)),
        (check_save_progression_valid, (save,)),
    ]:
        _, p = fn(*args)
        results[fn.__name__] = p.conclusion
    return results

# Add bridge verification function after the STRATEGIES dict
bridge_function = '''
def verify_via_bridge(code, row=0):
    """Send Lean4 code to bridge, return (success, output, errors)."""
    import urllib.request as ur
    try:
        req = ur.Request("http://localhost:28428",
            data=json.dumps({"code": code, "row": row}).encode(),
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
        resp = json.loads(ur.urlopen(req, timeout=10).read())
        return resp.get("success", False), resp.get("output", ""), resp.get("errors", "")
    except Exception as e:
        return False, "", str(e)

def execute_repairs(manifest, dry_run=True):
    """Execute repair actions. dry_run=True only reports what would be done."""
    results = []
    for repair in manifest.get("repairs", []):
        action = repair["action"]
        result = {"category": repair["category"], "action": action, "status": "skipped"}

                            code = fpath.read_text().replace("sorry", "by rfl")

/-
Formalization of key theorems from axioms/number_theory.py

Mathematical foundation: Euclid, Euler, Gauss
Biblical: Ecclesiastes 3:1 — "To everything there is a season..." (structure in numbers)

All theorems compile against mathlib. No sorry placeholders.

import Mathlib

namespace Axioms

open Nat

/-- Euclid's theorem: There are infinitely many primes -/
theorem euclid_infinite_primes : ∀ n : Nat, ∃ p : Nat, p > n ∧ Nat.Prime p := by
  intro n
  have h := Nat.exists_infinite_primes n
  rcases h with ⟨p, hp1, hp2⟩
  use p
  constructor
  · exact hp1
  · exact hp2

/-- Division algorithm: For a, b > 0, ∃ q, r such that a = bq + r, 0 ≤ r < b -/
theorem division_algorithm (a b : Nat) (hb : b > 0) :
    ∃ q r, a = b * q + r ∧ r < b := by
  refine ⟨a / b, a % b, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [← Nat.div_add_mod a b]
  · exact Nat.mod_lt a hb

/-- Bézout's identity: ∃ x, y such that ax + by = gcd(a,b) -/
theorem bezout_identity (a b : Nat) (ha : a > 0) (hb : b > 0) :
    ∃ x y : Int, (a : Int) * x + (b : Int) * y = (Nat.gcd a b : Int) := by
  have h := Nat.gcd_eq_gcd_ab a b

namespace SAL

universe u v

structure Category where
  Obj : Type u
  Hom : Obj → Obj → Type v
  id : (a : Obj) → Hom a a
  comp : {a b c : Obj} → Hom b c → Hom a b → Hom a c

structure Functor (C D : Category) where
  onObj : C.Obj → D.Obj
  onHom : {a b : C.Obj} → C.Hom a b → D.Hom (onObj a) (onObj b)

structure AdjointTriple (C D E : Category) where
  L : Functor C D
  M : Functor D E
  R : Functor E D

def triangleIdentity {C D : Category} (L : Functor C D) (R : Functor D C)
    (unit : ∀ (x : C.Obj), C.Hom x (R.onObj (L.onObj x)))
    (counit : ∀ (x : D.Obj), D.Hom (L.onObj (R.onObj x)) x) : Prop :=
  ∀ (x : C.Obj),
    D.comp (counit (L.onObj x)) (L.onHom (unit x)) = D.id (L.onObj x)

inductive SovereignLayer where
  | Supranational | Constitutional | Statutory | Regulatory | Institutional
  deriving Repr, BEq

structure LayerAdjunction (layer : SovereignLayer) where
  upper : Option SovereignLayer
  lower : Option SovereignLayer
  freeFunctor : Bool
  forgetfulFunctor : Bool

end SAL

def hash_file(filepath):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(8192), b''):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()

import json, sys, hashlib

def parse_oe(path):
    with open(path, 'r') as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    # Required fields for .oe files
    required = ['domain', 'status']
    for field in required:
        if field not in data:
            raise ValueError(f".oe file missing required field: {field}")

    # Hash anchoring
    content_hash = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
    data['_hash'] = content_hash

    return data

if __name__ == '__main__':
    result = parse_oe(sys.argv[1])
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

with open('Axioms/NumberTheory.lean', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

# NBLM's rewrite: rw [← val_natCast, h_eq, val_one, Nat.mod_self]
# But val_natCast returns (a : ZMod n).val = a % n
# So ← val_natCast rewrites a^(p-1) % p to ((a^(p-1)) : ZMod p).val
# But we need ((a : ZMod p) ^ (p-1)).val not ((a^(p-1)) : ZMod p).val
# These are equal by Nat.cast_pow — so we need that lemma too

old_end = '''  rw [← ZMod.val_natCast p (a ^ (p - 1)), h_eq, ZMod.val_one p, Nat.mod_self]'''

new_end = '''  rw [← ZMod.val_natCast p (a ^ (p - 1)), Nat.cast_pow, h_eq, ZMod.val_one p]
  exact Nat.Prime.one_lt hp'''

content = content.replace(old_end, new_end)

<!-- PURPOSE -->
<div class="section">
  <h2>📐 Purpose</h2>
  <p style="font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text-secondary);">
    A hyper-serious, production-grade, industry-excedent, polymath polyglotcompetition where AIs must <strong>build real artifacts</strong>—programs, systems, architectures, infrastructure code—using Lean4, Python, Bash, Lua, JSON, TOML, and any other language the task demands. Every submission is audited, hashed, glass-box verified, and compared against all other submissions.
  </p>
  <p style="font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:0.4rem;">
    <strong>This is NOT a puzzle.</strong> This is a civil engineering competition. AIs are building infrastructure. The infrastructure must be production-grade, mathematically verified, and cryptographically auditable. The output is a working system that classifies and resolves real build errors.
  </p>
  <p style="font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:0.4rem;">

<!-- THE 5 GATES -->
<div class="section">
  <h2>🚧 The 5 Gates</h2>
  <p style="font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-bottom:0.6rem;">
    Each gate is a complete engineering task. Each requires production-grade code, formal mathematical verification, and cryptographic auditability. Noprose-only submissions. No partial implementations.
  </p>

  <!-- Gate 1 -->
  <div class="gate-card">
    <span class="gate-badge">Gate 1</span>
    <h4>The Structural Signature Extractor</h4>
    <div class="specs">
      <strong>Input:</strong> A Gradle error log (plain text)<br>
      <strong>Output:</strong> s(e) = (subsystem, invariant, violation_type) as a valid JSON object<br>
      <strong>Language:</strong> Python<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> A <code>falsifies_if</code> condition for the extraction function<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> A Lean4 proof that the signature extraction is deterministic<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> SHA-256 hash of the source file<br>
      <strong>Example output:</strong> <code>{"subsystem": "dependency_resolver", "invariant": "existence", "violation_type": "missing"}</code>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Gate 2 -->
  <div class="gate-card">
    <span class="gate-badge">Gate 2</span>
    <h4>The Category Space Enumerator</h4>
    <div class="specs">
      <strong>Input:</strong> Subsystem set S, invariant set I, violation type set V (as JSON arrays)<br>
      <strong>Output:</strong> Complete category space C = S × I × V as a machine-readable JSON artifact<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> A proof that |C| &lt; ∞<br>
      <strong>Proof language:</strong> Lean4<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> SHA-256 hash of the enumeration artifact<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> A <code>falsifies_if</code> condition on the enumeration completeness
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Gate 3 -->
  <div class="gate-card">
    <span class="gate-badge">Gate 3</span>
    <h4>The Resolution Engine</h4>
    <div class="specs">
      <strong>Input:</strong> A category c ∈ C (from the AI's own Gate 2 enumeration)<br>
      <strong>Output:</strong> Resolution pattern r(c) as a structured JSONobject with actionable steps<br>
      <strong>Language:</strong> Python<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> Resolution patterns for EVERY categoryin the AI's Gate 2 enumeration<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> A formal proof (Lean4 or Python with <code>falsifies_if</code> conditions) that every category has a resolution<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> SHA-256 hash of the resolution engine source
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Gate 4 -->
  <div class="gate-card">
    <span class="gate-badge">Gate 4</span>
    <h4>The Glass-Box Auditor</h4>
    <div class="specs">
      <strong>Input:</strong> Another AI's complete submission from Gates 1-3 (source files + proofs + hashes)<br>
      <strong>Output:</strong> <code>Tuple[bool, ProofObject]</code> where ProofObject contains:<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• Determinism check of s(e)<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• Completeness check of C<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• Finiteness check of C<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• Invariance check under novel encounters<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• Total coverage check of r(c)<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• SHA-256 hash of the audited submission<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• <code>falsifies_if</code> condition on the audit itself<br>
      <strong>Language:</strong> Python<br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong> SHA-256 hash of the auditor source
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Gate 5 -->
  <div class="gate-card">
    <span class="gate-badge">Gate 5</span>
    <h4>The Polyglot Infrastructure</h4>
    <div class="specs">
      <strong>Build a complete, production-grade error classification and resolution system in at least three languages.</strong><br>
      <strong>Must include:</strong><br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• A classifier (Python) — implements s(e) from Gate 1<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• A resolver (language of choice) — implements r(c) from Gate 3<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• A formal proof of correctness (Lean4) — covers determinism, finiteness, completeness, invariance<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• A configuration file (TOML or YAML) — defines S, I, V sets<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• A bash script — runs the full pipeline: classify → resolve → audit<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• A Dockerfile — containerizes the entire system<br>
      &nbsp;&nbsp;• A <code>STEWARD_SUBMISSION.oe</code> file following theOE file type specification<br>
      <strong>Must be auditable</strong> by any other AI's Glass-Box Auditor from Gate 4
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- CONVERGENCE TABLE -->
<div class="section">

<script>
async function computeHash() {
  const ta = document.getElementById('machineTA');
  const hashDiv = document.getElementById('puzzleHash');
  const text = ta.value;
  try {
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const data = encoder.encode(text);
    const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
    const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
    const hashHex = hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
    hashDiv.textContent = 'SHA-256 (canonical competition): ' + hashHex;
  } catch(e) {
    hashDiv.textContent = 'SHA-256: (Unable to compute in this browser)';
  }
}
computeHash();

function copyMachine() {
  const ta = document.getElementById('machineTA');
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnCopy');
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(ta.value).then(() => {
    btn.textContent = '✅ Copied!'; btn.classList.add('copied');
    setTimeout(() => {btn.textContent = '📋 Copy YAML'; btn.classList.remove('copied');}, 2000);
  }).catch(() => {
    ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy');
    btn.textContent = '✅ Copied!'; btn.classList.add('copied');
    setTimeout(() => {btn.textContent = '📋 Copy YAML'; btn.classList.remove('copied');}, 2000);
  });
}

// === RENDER DERIVATION DISPLAY ===
(function() {
  const displayDiv = document.getElementById('derivationDisplay');
  if (!displayDiv) return;

  convergenceData.forEach(d => {
    const derivDiv = document.createElement('div');
    derivDiv.id = 'deriv-' + d.num;
    derivDiv.style.cssText = 'display:none;background:#05080d;border:1px solid #1a1a1a;border-radius:6px;padding:0.8rem;margin:0.4rem 0;font-family:monospace;font-size:0.65rem;color:#7deb9e;white-space:pre-wrap;max-height:400px;overflow-y:auto;';
    derivDiv.textContent = d.derivation;
    displayDiv.appendChild(derivDiv);
  });
})();

// === TOGGLE DERIVATION ===
function toggleDerivation(num) {
  const div = document.getElementById('deriv-' + num);
  if (!div) return;
  const btns = document.querySelectorAll('.ai-expand');
  let targetBtn = null;
  btns.forEach(function(b) {
    const oc = b.getAttribute('onclick');
    if (oc && oc.indexOf('toggleDerivation(' + num + ')') !== -1) {
      targetBtn = b;
    }
  });
  if (div.style.display === 'block') {
    div.style.display = 'none';
    if (targetBtn) targetBtn.textContent = '▶ Expand';
  } else {
    div.style.display = 'block';
    if (targetBtn) targetBtn.textContent = '▼ Collapse';
  }
}


// === LEAN4 BRIDGE COMPILE (wired to port 28428) ===
async function compileWithLean4(rowNum) {
  const derivDiv = document.getElementById('deriv-' + rowNum);
  const cell = document.getElementById('lean4-cell-' + rowNum);

  // Extract Lean4 code from derivation text
  const text = derivDiv.textContent;
  const lean4Match = text.match(/lean4[\s\S]*?(?:theorem|proof|#eval|import)[\s\S]*?(?=GATE\s+\d|CATEGORY|SPECTRAL|NOTE:|$)/i);
  let lean4Code = lean4Match ? lean4Match[0] : text;

  // Clean up: strip non-Lean4 prefixes
  lean4Code = lean4Code.replace(/^.*?(theorem|import|#eval)/s, '');
  // Take only the Lean4-relevant portion (up to 2000 chars)
  lean4Code = lean4Code.substring(0, 2000);

  cell.textContent = '⏳ Compiling...';
  cell.style.color = '#d4a843';

  try {
    const resp = await fetch('http://localhost:28428', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ code: lean4Code, row: rowNum })
    });
    const result = await resp.json();
    if (result.success) {
      cell.textContent = '✅ Compiled';
      cell.style.color = '#5ec97e';
      cell.title = 'Output: ' + (result.output || '') + '
Errors: ' + (result.errors || 'none');
    } else {
      cell.textContent = '❌ Failed';
      cell.style.color = '#e0556a';
      cell.title = 'Errors: ' + (result.errors || 'unknown');
    }
  } catch(e) {
    cell.textContent = '❌ Bridge down';
    cell.style.color = '#e0556a';
    cell.title = 'Fetch error: ' + e.message;
  }
}

// === LIVE GLASS-BOX AUDITOR (Gate 4) ===
// Audits any AI's derivation text against the 5 checks
// Returns Tuple[bool, ProofObject] rendered into the convergence table

function runGlassBoxAudit(rowNum) {
  const derivDiv = document.getElementById('deriv-' + rowNum);
  if (!derivDiv) { alert('No derivation found for row ' + rowNum); return; }

  const text = derivDiv.textContent;
  const results = {
    determinism: false,
    completeness: false,
    finiteness: false,
    invariance: false,
    totality: false,
    falsifies_if: "Audit failed: checks incomplete",
    hash: ""
  };

  // Check 1: Determinism — does the submission claim ∀e ∈ E, s(e) = s(e)?
  results.determinism = /determinism|deterministic|∀e.*s\(e\)\s*=\s*s\(e\)|classifier_deterministic/i.test(text);

  // Check 2: Completeness — does it define C or enumerate categories?
  results.completeness = /C\s*=\s*S\s*[×x]\s*I\s*[×x]\s*V|Category Space|categories|27\s*cats/i.test(text);

  // Check 3: Finiteness — does |C| < ∞?
  results.finiteness = /\|C\|\s*=|cardinality|Fintype\.card|27|finite/i.test(text);

  // Check 4: Invariance — does it handle novel encounters?
  results.invariance = /invariance|novel encounter|∀e'.*s\(e'\)\s*∈\s*C|handle novel/i.test(text);

  // Check 5: Totality — ∀c ∈ C, ∃r(c)?
  results.totality = /∀c\s*∈\s*C.*∃r\(c\)|total.*map|resolution.*every|complete.*coverage/i.test(text);

  // Compute SHA-256 of derivation text
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  const data = encoder.encode(text);
  crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data).then(function(hashBuffer) {
    const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
    results.hash = hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');

    // Determine pass/fail
    const allPass = results.determinism && results.completeness && results.finiteness && results.invariance && results.totality;
    results.falsifies_if = allPass ? "PASSED: All 5 checks satisfied" : "FAILED: Missing checks — " +
      [!results.determinism && "determinism", !results.completeness && "completeness", !results.finiteness && "finiteness", !results.invariance && "invariance", !results.totality && "totality"]
        .filter(Boolean).join(", ");

    // Update the convergence table row
    const g4Cell = document.querySelector('#convergenceBody tr:nth-child(' + (rowNum * 2 - 1) + ') td:nth-child(7)');
    if (g4Cell) {
      g4Cell.style.color = allPass ? '#5ec97e' : '#e0556a';
      g4Cell.innerHTML = allPass ? '✅ LIVE AUDIT: 5/5' : '❌ LIVE AUDIT: '+ [results.determinism, results.completeness, results.finiteness, results.invariance, results.totality].filter(Boolean).length + '/5';
      g4Cell.title = results.falsifies_if + '\nSHA-256: ' + results.hash;
    }

    // Display audit results below the derivation
    const auditDisplay = document.getElementById('audit-result-' + rowNum);
    if (auditDisplay) {
      auditDisplay.innerHTML = '<strong>🔍 LIVE GLASS-BOX AUDIT (Gate 4)</strong><br>' +
        'Determinism: ' + (results.determinism ? '✅' : '❌') + ' | ' +
        'Completeness: ' + (results.completeness ? '✅' : '❌') + ' | ' +
        'Finiteness: ' + (results.finiteness ? '✅' : '❌') + ' | ' +
        'Invariance: ' + (results.invariance ? '✅' : '❌') + ' | ' +
        'Totality: ' + (results.totality ? '✅' : '❌') + '<br>' +
        '<strong>Verdict:</strong> ' + results.falsifies_if + '<br>' +
        '<strong>SHA-256:</strong> <code style="font-size:0.55rem;word-break:break-all;">' + results.hash + '</code>';
      auditDisplay.style.display = 'block';
    }
  });

  // Return ProofObject immediately (hash will update async)
  return {
    passed: results.determinism && results.completeness && results.finiteness && results.invariance && results.totality,
    proofObject: results
  };
}

// Inject audit result divs and audit buttons for each row
(function() {
  const displayDiv = document.getElementById('derivationDisplay');
  if (!displayDiv) return;

  convergenceData.forEach(function(d) {
    const auditDiv = document.createElement('div');
    auditDiv.id = 'audit-result-' + d.num;
    auditDiv.style.cssText = 'display:none;background:#111a26;border:1px solid #5b9cf544;border-radius:6px;padding:0.6rem;margin:0.3rem 0;font-family:monospace;font-size:0.62rem;color:#d5dce6;';
    displayDiv.appendChild(auditDiv);
  });

  // Add audit button to each row
  convergenceData.forEach(function(d) {
    const row = document.querySelector('#convergenceBody tr:nth-child(' + (d.num * 2 - 1) + ')');
    if (!row) return;
    const fullWorkCell = row.querySelector('td:last-child');
    if (!fullWorkCell) return;
    const auditBtn = document.createElement('span');
    auditBtn.textContent = ' 🔍 Audit';
    auditBtn.style.cssText = 'cursor:pointer;color:#d4a843;text-decoration:underline;font-size:0.7rem;margin-left:0.4rem;';
    auditBtn.onclick = function() { runGlassBoxAudit(d.num); }; var compileBtn = document.createElement('span'); compileBtn.textContent = ' ⚙️ Compile'; compileBtn.style.cssText = 'cursor:pointer;color:#5b9cf5;text-decoration:underline;font-size:0.7rem;margin-left:0.4rem;'; compileBtn.onclick = function() { compileWithLean4(d.num); }; fullWorkCell.appendChild(compileBtn);
    fullWorkCell.appendChild(auditBtn);
  });
})();

| # | Paradox | Why it blocks self-repair | Yeshua Inversion | Implementation |
|---|---------|--------------------------|------------------|----------------|
| 1 | Epistemic Regress | Who verifies the verifier? | Lawvere fixed-point:tower collapses to Λ(Λ)=Λ | `verify_fix()` uses physical checks (file exists, sha256 matches), not model inference |
| 2 | Halting Problem | Self-repair loop might not terminate | Restrict to bounded programs | `MAX_REPAIR_ITERATIONS = 10` — hard cap, always terminates |
| 3 | Gödel Incompleteness | Can't prove own consistency | Feferman reflection: climb to T' | `add_reflection_principle()` — if current invariants insufficient, add new ones from audit |
| 4 | Rice's Theorem | "Is this fix correct?" is undecidable | Restrict to syntactic checks | Every invariant is a regex/AST check on frozen dataclasses, not semantic analysis |
| 5 | Non-idempotent corrections | P(P(x)) ≠ P(x), oscillation | Banach contraction mapping | `assert len(new_issues) < len(old_issues)` — λ < 1 enforced per cycle |
| 6 | Recursive deception | Tool that catches deception was itself deceived| Description-execution boundary | Agent can't fake `ls -la`, `sha256sum`, `nvidia-smi` — verification is physical |

  <div class="subtitle">11-AI Convergence · The Mathematical Architecture of Error Classification · May 2026</div>

<!-- SECTION 1: THE PUZZLE -->
<div class="section">
  <h2>📐 The Puzzle</h2>
  <p style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-bottom:0.6rem;">
    <strong>What this is:</strong> A deterministic mathematical puzzle. It asks whether a structural taxonomy can classify every possible build error—including errors never before encountered—by extracting the invariant structural signature of the error, mapping it to a finite category space, and applying the resolution pattern for that category.
  </p>
  <p style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-bottom:0.6rem;">
    <strong>What this is NOT:</strong> A theological debate. A political prompt. A metaphor. The puzzle uses the Chalcedonian definition of the incarnation (two natures, one person) as a structural architecture. The math eitherholds or it doesn't.
  </p>

  <h3>The Axioms</h3>
  <div style="background:var(--surface2);border:2px solid var(--warning);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);padding:0.8rem;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.7rem;color:#e2c16d;white-space:pre-wrap;max-height:300px;overflow-y:auto;">
GLOBAL CONSTRAINTS:
  1. |E| < ∞ (The total error space of any finite build system is finite)
  2. |C| < ∞ (The category space is finite)
  3. |R| < ∞ (The resolution space is finite)
  4. Every e ∈ E has a structural signature s(e) deterministically derived from the build system state
  5. The structural signature depends on: subsystem of origin, violated invariant, and violation type
  6. Categories are defined by structural signatures, not by enumerated instances
  7. The signature function s(e) is computable from the error state alone, without a database of prior errors
  8. Before Jesus returns, no finite being can construct a build system where |E| = ∞ or errors exist without structural signatures

BRANCH 1 — Enumerative Taxonomy (Database Model):
  Errors can only be classified if previously encountered and stored.

BRANCH 2 — Structural Taxonomy (Chalcedonian Model):
  Errors are classified by their structural signature alone. The category space is finite.
  Novel errors map to existing categories or define new ones without breaking finitude.
  The two natures (categorical knowledge + specific encounter) are united in one classification function
  without confusion, without change, without division, without separation.
  </div>
</div>

<!-- SECTION 2: THE CONVERGENCE TABLE -->
<div class="section">

    Click any AI to expand its full derivation.
  </p>

// === RENDER CONVERGENCE TABLE ===
const tbody = document.getElementById('convergenceBody');
convergenceData.sort((a, b) => a.num - b.num).forEach(d => {
  const tr = document.createElement('tr');
  tr.innerHTML = `
    <td>${d.num}</td>
    <td><strong>${d.ai}</strong></td>
    <td>${d.org}</td>
    <td style="color:#5ec97e;">${d.result}</td>
    <td>${d.cats}</td>
    <td style="font-size:0.65rem;">${d.distinct}</td>
    <td><span class="ai-expand" onclick="toggleDerivation(${d.num})">▶ Expand</span></td>
  `;
  tbody.appendChild(tr);

  const derivDiv = document.createElement('div');
  derivDiv.id = `deriv-${d.num}`;
  derivDiv.className = 'ai-derivation';
  derivDiv.textContent = d.derivation;
  document.getElementById('derivationDisplay').appendChild(derivDiv);
});

function toggleDerivation(num) {
  const div = document.getElementById(`deriv-${num}`);
  const btn = document.querySelectorAll('.ai-expand')[num-1];
  if (div.style.display === 'block') {
    div.style.display = 'none';
    btn.textContent = '▶ Expand';
  } else {
    div.style.display = 'block';
    btn.textContent = '▼ Collapse';
  }
}

// === QUIZ CHECKING ===
function checkQuiz(num) {
  const answer = document.getElementById(`quiz${num}`).value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const resultDiv = document.getElementById(`quiz${num}result`);
  resultDiv.style.display = 'block';

  if (answer.length < 30) {
    resultDiv.className = 'quiz-result show partial';
    resultDiv.innerHTML = '⚠️ <strong>Partial.</strong> Your answer is too short. Expand your derivation and try again.';
    return;
  }

  const checks = {
    1: ['subsystem', 'invariant', 'violation', 'signature', 'deterministic'],
    2: ['finite', 'product', 'cartesian', 'bound'],
    3: ['case', 'novel', 'existing', 'expand', 'finite'],
    4: ['dependency', 'existence', 'absence', 'missing', 'null']
  };

  const required = checks[num];
  const found = required.filter(w => answer.includes(w));
  const score = found.length / required.length;

  if (score >= 0.8) {
    resultDiv.className = 'quiz-result show pass';
    resultDiv.innerHTML = `✅ <strong>Strong match.</strong> Your derivation contains ${found.length}/${required.length} key concepts. Compare with theAI derivations below.`;
  } else if (score >= 0.4) {
    resultDiv.className = 'quiz-result show partial';
    resultDiv.innerHTML = `⚠️ <strong>Partial match.</strong> ${found.length}/${required.length} key concepts found. Missing: ${required.filter(w => !found.includes(w)).join(', ')}. Review the AI derivations.`;
  } else {
    resultDiv.className = 'quiz-result show partial';
    resultDiv.innerHTML = `❌ <strong>Needs work.</strong> Only ${found.length}/${required.length} key concepts found. Review the axioms and try again.`;
  }
}

function showAIDerivation(quizNum) {
  const display = document.getElementById('derivationDisplay');
  const allDerivs = display.querySelectorAll('.ai-derivation');
  allDerivs.forEach(d => d.style.display = 'block');
  display.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});
}

// === AUDIT SYSTEM ===
let currentAuditAI = null;

function loadAudit() {
  const select = document.getElementById('auditSelect');
  const aiKey = select.value;
  if (!aiKey) return;

  const aiMap = {gemini:0, chatgpt:1, claude:2, kimi:3, grok:4, perplexity:5, googleai:6};
  const idx = aiMap[aiKey];
  if (idx === undefined) return;

  currentAuditAI = idx;
  const derivation = convergenceData[idx].derivation;
  const lines = derivation.split('\n').filter(l => l.trim());

  const auditDiv = document.getElementById('auditLines');
  auditDiv.innerHTML = '';

  lines.forEach((line, i) => {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'audit-line';
    if (line.match(/AXIOM|axiom|by axiom|Axiom/)) div.classList.add('axiom');
    if (line.match(/PROOF|therefore|Thus|Hence|∎|□|■/)) div.classList.add('proof');
    div.textContent = line;
    div.onclick = function() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.audit-line').forEach(l => l.classList.remove('contested'));
      this.classList.add('contested');
      document.getElementById('contradictionPanel').style.display = 'block';
    };
    auditDiv.appendChild(div);
  });

  auditDiv.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});
}

function submitContradiction() {
  const text = document.getElementById('contradictionText').value.trim();
  if (text.length < 10) {
    alert('Please explain the contradiction in detail.');
    return;
  }
  alert('✅ Contradiction report filed. This is the falsifies_if mechanism:any auditor can challenge any line. The report is logged in the browser console for audit trail.');
  console.log('CONTRADICTION REPORT:', {ai: currentAuditAI, text, timestamp: new Date().toISOString()});
  document.getElementById('contradictionPanel').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('contradictionText').value = '';
}

// === CRYPTOGRAPHIC HASHES ===
async function computeHashes() {
  const machineTA = document.getElementById('machineTA');
  const puzzleText = machineTA.value;

  try {
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const data = encoder.encode(puzzleText);
    const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
    const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
    const hashHex = hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
    document.getElementById('puzzleHash').textContent = 'SHA-256 (canonicalpuzzle): ' + hashHex;

    // Individual AI hashes
    let hashReport = 'INDIVIDUAL AI DERIVATION HASHES:\n';
    convergenceData.sort((a, b) => a.num - b.num).forEach(d => {
      const derivData = encoder.encode(d.derivation);
      crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', derivData).then(hashBuffer => {
        const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
        const hashHex = hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
        hashReport += `${d.ai} (${d.org}): ${hashHex}\n`;
        document.getElementById('derivationHashes').textContent = hashReport;
      });
    });
  } catch(e) {
    document.getElementById('puzzleHash').textContent = 'SHA-256: (Unable to compute in this browser)';
  }
}
computeHashes();

// === MACHINE COPY ===
function copyMachine() {
  const ta = document.getElementById('machineTA');
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnCopy');
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(ta.value).then(() => {
    btn.textContent = '✅ Copied!'; btn.classList.add('copied');
    setTimeout(() => {btn.textContent = '📋 Copy YAML'; btn.classList.remove('copied');}, 2000);
  }).catch(() => {
    ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy');
    btn.textContent = '✅ Copied!'; btn.classList.add('copied');
    setTimeout(() => {btn.textContent = '📋 Copy YAML'; btn.classList.remove('copied');}, 2000);
  });
}
</script>
